lines:
  - id: f8863.line7

    label: "Multiply line 1 by line 6. Caution: If you were under age 24 at the end of the year and meet the conditions described in the instructions, you can't take the refundable American opportunity credit; skip line 8, enter the amount from line 7 on line 9, and check this box"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: prorate
      amount: f8863.line1
      part: f8863.line4
      whole: f8863.line5
      places: 3
    refuses: [f8863.ban_if_aoc]
    shown_when: f8863.show_after4
    note: "Printed line 6 is not among the requested lines. Its rule is: if line 4 is equal to or more than line 5, enter 1.000; otherwise divide line 4 by line 5, rounded to at least three places. Line 7 multiplies line 1 by that result, so it is written as one prorate of line 1 by line 4 over line 5. The text says 'at least three places', which leaves the number open; three places was chosen. The prorate cap at 1 gives the 1.000 branch. If line 4 is zero (the line 4 STOP, no education credit), the ratio is 0 and line 7 is 0. The under-24 caution affects only lines 8 and 9 and the box, not this amount."
  - id: f8863.ban_if_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Ban on claiming the American opportunity credit applies (Form 8862 required, or within the 2-year or 10-year period after a final determination), and an American opportunity credit is being claimed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8863.paid, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8863.ban, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [f8863.line1, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The pages do not define this interface directly. Reading chosen from the i8863 text: two passages bar the American opportunity credit. The 'Form 8862 may be required' reminder requires Form 8862 after a denial or reduction for a tax year after 2015. The Caution under American Opportunity Credit forbids a claim for 2 years after a reckless-or-intentional determination, or 10 years after a fraud determination. This line treats either bar as applying only when education expenses were paid and some American opportunity credit amount (Part I, line 1) is being claimed. The lifetime learning credit is unaffected."

Second transcription's note: The pages do not define this interface directly. Reading chosen from the i8863 text: two passages bar the American opportunity credit. The 'Form 8862 may be required' reminder requires Form 8862 after a denial or reduction for a tax year after 2015. The Caution under American Opportunity Credit forbids a claim for 2 years after a reckless-or-intentional determination, or 10 years after a fraud determination. This line treats either bar as applying only when education expenses were paid and some American opportunity credit amount (Part I, line 1) is being claimed. The lifetime learning credit is unaffected.

Second transcription's note: Printed line 6 is not among the requested lines. Its rule is: if line 4 is equal to or more than line 5, enter 1.000; otherwise divide line 4 by line 5, rounded to at least three places. Line 7 multiplies line 1 by that result, so it is written as one prorate of line 1 by line 4 over line 5. The text says 'at least three places', which leaves the number open; three places was chosen. The prorate cap at 1 gives the 1.000 branch. If line 4 is zero (the line 4 STOP, no education credit), the ratio is 0 and line 7 is 0. The under-24 caution affects only lines 8 and 9 and the box, not this amount.
